```
def z_objective(z, n, beta, maybe exact):
    p = softmax(z)
    c = fft_convolution(p)
    # smooth G = m + logsumexp(beta(c-m))/beta
    if m <=0: return 0, zeros
    G = m + logsumexp(beta*(c-m))/beta
    # objective = n*G (score smooth approx 2n*max, since score=2n max; actual score =? Wait p sum=1, score=2n*max. Minimize n*G equivalent half score. okay)
    grad_p = 2 corr_pw(p, w)
    grad_p -= mean(grad_p)  # for softmax? No, for softmax z we need p*(grad_p - mean(grad_p))
    grad_z = p * (grad_p - mean(grad_p))
    return n*G, grad_z
```

```
def run_lbfgs(func, x0, bounds, deadline, maxiter=None, print_prefix=''):
    if not HAVE_SCIPY: return x0
    if x0.size==0: return x0
    x0=np.clip(x0,bounds)
    options={'maxiter': maxiter or 300,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-5,'maxls':50,'maxcor':20}
    best_x=x0.copy(); best_f=func(x0)[0]
    # We can use a wrapper that times and stops? SciPy doesn't accept callback returning stop? It has callback maybe but not stop. We can set maxiter and check time between? Can't interrupt inside. We can estimate time per iteration by first call? Or use wrapper that repeats? Better: use manual progress via `options['maxiter']` and after each? no.
```

```
class TimeLimitError(Exception): pass
state={'best_f':inf,'best_x':None}
def wrapped_func(x):
   if time.monotonic() > limit:
       raise TimeLimitError
   f,g=func(x)
   if f<state best: store x copy
   return f,g
try:
  res=minimize(wrapped_func,...)
  x=res.x
except TimeLimitError:
  x=state.best_x or x0
```

Project simplex sorting:
```
def project_simplex(v, target=1):
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    css=np.cumsum(u)
    rho=np.nonzero(u - (css - target)/np.arange(1,len(u)+1) > 0)[0]
    if len(rho)==0: return zeros? 
    rho=rho[-1]
    tau=(u[:rho+1].sum()-target)/(rho+1)
    return np.maximum(v-tau,0)
```
This is O(n log n). Use if target. If v all same, works. Need target positive.
For n large, sort per projection may be okay if iterations not too many. In L-BFGS no projection.

For manual polish, maybe use a **soft projection** `q = np.maximum(p - alpha*grad, 0); q /= q.sum()` which is not exact but okay (Bregman?) If q sum not 1, normalize. This is cheap O(n) and keeps simplex approximate. It may step out and renormalize, not true projection but can work. Prior program used exact. We can use exact for robust.

Maybe use `project_simplex` with binary search but optimize with `np.searchsorted`? Sorting simpler.

```
def build_basis(n,rng):
 x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
 d=[]
 d.append(x)
 d.append((x-0.5)**2)
 d.append((x-0.5)**3)
 d.append((x-0.5)**4)
 d.append(x*(1-x))
 d.append(x*x*(1-x)*(1-x))
 d.append(np.cos(np.pi*x))
 d.append(np.cos(2*np.pi*x))
 d.append(np.sin(np.pi*x))
 d.append(np.sin(2*np.pi*x))
 # random smooth modes
 m = rng.normal(size=16); amplitudes decay 1/(i+1)^1.5; z = ifft? produce real.
 basis = np.vstack(d[:12])
 # center? constant not needed.
 return basis
```

```
def lbfgs_callback(x, ctx):
    if time.monotonic() > ctx['deadline']:
        raise TimeLimitError
    f,g = ctx['func'](x)
    if np.isfinite(f) and f < ctx.get('best_f',inf): ctx['best_x']=x.copy(); ctx['best_f']=float(f)
    if not np.isfinite(f):
       f = 1e100
       g = np.zeros_like(x)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
       g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
    return f,g
```

```
def softmax(z):
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)): z=np.zeros_like(z)
   z=z-z.max()
   exp=np.exp(z)
   s=exp.sum()
   if s<=0 or not finite: uniform
   return exp/s
```

Convolution:
```
def fft_convolution(a):
    a=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.empty(0)
    if n==1: return np.array([a[0]*a[0]])
    need=2*n-1
    size=1
    while size<need: size <<=1
    F=np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    b=np.fft.irfft(F*F, size)[:need]
    # roundoff can be small negative; also values maybe tiny > true
    b=np.ascontiguousarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    # Because all inputs nonnegative, true coefficients nonnegative. But if input p has tiny negative? no.
    b[np.abs(b) < 1e-15 * (np.max(np.abs(b)) if b.size else 1)] = 0? This may cost. Simpler np.maximum(b,0.0)
    return np.maximum(b,0.0)
```
Clipping negative okay. But if FFT roundoff makes a true small positive negative, clipped 0 underestimates M by tiny; not issue.

`corr_pw`:
```
def corr_pw(p,w):
    n=p.size; m=w.size
    if n==0 or m==0: return zeros
    need=n+m-1
    size=1
    while size<need: size <<=1
    P=np.fft.rfft(p,size)
    WR=np.fft.rfft(w[::-1], size)
    C=np.fft.irfft(P*WR, size)[:need]
    s=C[m-n:m][::-1].copy()
    # Due to roundoff could negative; derivative of nonnegative c? For gradient, negative small okay; previous max(0). We can not max because gradient of max? But s should be nonnegative (p,w nonneg). Clip to 0.
    return np.maximum(s,0.0)
```
Check index: previous `s = C[m - n : m][::-1]`. Need for n=1,m=1 need=1 size1, C[0], m-n:m=0:1, reverse [C0]. Good. For p length n,w length 2n-1, need=3n-2? Wait convolution p (n) and w_rev (m) length n+m-1 =3n-2. Size power. s_i = Σ_j p_j w_{i+j}, i=0..n-1. This is cross-correlation. The middle slice formula: C[k] =Σ_{j} p_j w_rev_{k-j} =Σ_j p_j w_{m-1 - (k-j)} =Σ p_j w_{m-1-k+j}. We want i: Σ p_j w_{i+j}. Set m-1-k = i => k=m-1-i. For i=0..n-1, k=m-1-i ranges m-1 down m-n. So slice C[m-n:m] reversed gives k=m-1...m-n. Good.

`smooth_max_details(c,beta)`:
```
def smooth_max_details(c,beta):
    m=c.size
    if m==0: return inf, empty, inf
    M=float(np.max(c))
    if not finite(M): return inf, zeros, inf
    if M<=0 or beta<=0:
       w=ones/m; return 0,w,0
    x=beta*(c-M)
    # For numerical, if beta=0? handled.
    x=x - np.max(x) # max is 0 but if due
    x=np.clip(x,-745,0)
    e=np.exp(x)
    Z=e.sum()
    if Z<=0 or not finite(Z): weights at argmax
    w=e/Z
    G=M + np.log(Z)/beta
    return float(G), w, M
```
If beta large and c has multiple exact max, Z active_count. G slightly >M.

`fast_score(p)` returns exact score and M maybe.
```
def fast_score(p):
   p=np.asarray(p)
   n=p.size
   if n==0: return inf, inf
   S=p.sum()
   if S<0.01? But our p sum1. For fast, scale invariant: normalize? We'll assume sum1. But if not, score=2*n*max/ S^2.
   c=fft_convolution(p)
   M=max(c)
   score=2*n*M/(S*S)
   return score, M
```
For normalization, we ensure sum1. Use `S` to be safe.

`build_basis(n, rng=None)`:
Need no random? We can include deterministic random if rng. Basis shape (d,n). Avoid constant. Use memory okay.
```
def build_basis(n, rng=None):
   x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
   c = x-0.5
   feats=[c, c*c, c*c*c, c**4, x*(1-x), x*x*(1-x)*(1-x), np.cos(np.pi*x), np.sin(np.pi*x), np.cos(2*np.pi*x), np.sin(2*np.pi*x)]
   # maybe add edge ramp: x - 0.5 (c) and x^2? c^2 covers; add x^2? c*c = x^2 - x + .25, same as x^2 and -x; okay.
   # add c*x? =?
   feats = [f for f in feats if finite]
   if rng is not None:
      # lowpass random 1
      size=n
      z=np.fft.rfft? simpler generate time-domain noise smoothed by FFT:
      noise=rng.normal(size=n)
      # apply lowpass in fft
      F=np.fft.rfft(noise); F*= 1/np.sqrt(1+ (np.arange(len(F))/max(1,n/16))**2)
      noise=np.fft.irfft(F,n)
      feats.append(noise/np.std(noise) if std>0 else noise)
   basis=np.vstack(feats).astype(np.float64)
   # center each feature: basis -= mean axis1 to align with softmax const
   basis -= basis.mean(axis=1,keepdims=True)
   # scale each feature to max abs ~1
   for i in range(basis.shape[0]):
      mx=np.max(np.abs(basis[i]))
      if mx>0: basis[i]/=mx
   return basis
```
No closures. If rng None no random.

Now, block to p and objective need ensure p sum exactly 1. Let's implement:
```
def block_to_p(n,B,c):
   L=n//B
   if B<=1: return np.full(n,1/n)
   c=np.asarray(c).reshape(-1)
   z=np.zeros(B)
   m=min(B-1,c.size); z[:m]=c[:m]
   z-=z.max()
   wb=np.exp(z)
   s=wb.sum()
   if s<=0: wb=np.ones(B)/B else wb/=s
   H=wb/L
   return np.repeat(H,L)
```
If n%B !=0, we only call with divisors. For upgrade maybe not. Ensure choose B dividing N.

Block objective gradient: Need `Gsum` length B. `g=2*corr_pw(p,w)`; `Gsum = g.reshape(B,L).sum(axis=1)`; `Wavg = dot(wb,Gsum)`; `gradz = (wb / L)*(Gsum - Wavg); grad = 2.0 * n * gradz[:-1]`. Wait if block_to_p uses z last fixed 0, the derivative for B-1 variables as above. But if we use objective f=2nG, multiply 2n. Good. b before max? yes.

```
def refine_sequence(p0, n, deadline, rng, label):
   # p0 normalized
   best_p = p0.copy(); best_exact=fast_score(p0)[0]
   # Maybe smooth polish with each start? We'll use L-BFGS full.
   # Bound z and beta stages.
   z = z_from_p(p0)
   bounds=[(-Z_BOUND,Z_BOUND)]*n
   betas = [50000.0, 200000.0, 1000000.0] # if n large? c~1/n, beta*c ~ beta/n. For n=16384: 3,12,61. For n=8192:6,24,122. Good. For n=4096:12,48,244. Maybe beta high 3e6.
   # time fractions: [0.3,0.4,0.3]
   for i,beta in enumerate(betas):
       rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
       if rem<10: break
       alloc=rem*frac
       state={'best_p':best_p.copy(),'best_exact':best_exact}
       state.update({'best_z':z.copy(), 'best_f':...})
       x = run_lbfgs(z, bounds, full_callback, ctx, deadline=start+alloc, maxiter=??? )
       # update best from state
       if state best exact < best_exact: best_p=state['best_p']; best_exact=...
       # choose z for next: maybe state best_z if finite else x clipped
       z = np.asarray(state.get('best_z', x)).copy(); z=np.clip(z,-Z,Z); z-=z.mean(); # softmax invariant
   # manual polish with projected gradient if time
   if time<deadline-10:
       p, sc = polish_grad(best_p, deadline, rng, beta=1000000)
       if sc<best_exact: best_p=sc; best_exact=sc
   return best_p,best_exact
```

```
def block_init_from_p(p,B):
   n=p.size; L=n//B
   block_means = p.reshape(B,L).mean(axis=1)
   # log relative, last fixed 0: relative = log(block_means/block_means[-1])? But if last zero, handle.
```

```
def projected_polish(p, deadline, beta):
    p=normalize_sequence(p)
    best_p=p.copy(); best_exact=fast_score(p)[0]
    n=p.size
    step=0.5
    no=0
    it=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       c,M=..., G,w,_=smooth
       g=2*corr; grad = g - mean(g)
       # step size adapt: use line search with smooth objective
       accepted=False
       for mult in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01):
           alpha=step*mult
           q=project_simplex(p - alpha*grad)
           # quick smooth score: c_q=fft_convolution(q); Gq,_=smooth; fq=2nGq
           if fq < current_f -1e-12:
              p=q; current_f=fq; current_M=...; accepted=True; update best exact; break
       if not accepted: step*=0.5; no+=1; if no>100 break
       else: step=min(2,step*1.2); no=0
       if it%10==0 update exact maybe
    return best_p,best_exact
```
This uses many FFTs. Reserve maybe 30-60s. Could be beneficial. But if L-BFGS good, maybe skip to save time for larger N. We'll call only if time remains after main.

```
def fallback_gradient_descent(p, deadline, beta):
    p=normalize(p); best_p=p; best_exact=fast_score(p)[0]; step=1.0
    while time<deadline:
       c=fft_convolution(p); G,w,M=smooth
       f=2*n*G
       g=2*corr; grad=g-mean
       # projected step no line search: try few
       accepted=False
       for a in (step,step*0.5,step*0.25,step*0.1):
          q=project_simplex(p - a*grad)
          cq=fft_convolution(q); Gq,_,_=smooth; fq=2nGq
          if fq < f -1e-12:
             p=q; f=fq; accepted=True; update; break
       if not accepted: step*=0.5; if step<1e-6 break
       else: step=min(2,step*1.1)
    return best_p,best_exact
```

`score_top(p_list, count)`:
```
def score_top(p_list, count):
    scored=[]
    for p in p_list:
        sc, M = fast_score(normalize_sequence(p))
        if np.isfinite(sc): scored.append([sc,p])
    available=scored
    selected=[]
    for _ in range(min(count,len(available))):
        bi=0
        for i in range(1,len(available)):
            if available[i][0] < available[bi][0]: bi=i
        selected.append(available[bi][1])
        del available[bi]
    return selected
```
No lambda.

`general run_lbfgs_x` catch exceptions:
```
def run_lbfgs_x(x0, bounds, callback, ctx, maxiter):
    x0=np.asarray(x0,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if x0.size==0: return x0
    if bounds is not None and len(bounds)!=x0.size: bounds=None
    ctx['deadline'] = float(ctx.get('deadline', time.monotonic()))
    ctx['best_f']=float('inf'); ctx['best_x']=x0.copy()
    if not HAVE_SCIPY:
       try: f,g=callback(x0,ctx)
       except Exception: return x0
       return ctx.get('best_x',x0)
    options={'maxiter': int(maxiter), 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-5, 'maxls':40, 'maxcor':20}
    try:
       res=minimize(callback, x0, args=(ctx,), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=bounds, options=options)
       if hasattr(res,'x') and res.x is not None:
           x=np.asarray(res.x,dtype=np.float64)
           if np.all(np.isfinite(x)): return x
     except TimeLimitError:
       pass
     except Exception:
       pass
    bx=ctx.get('best_x')
    if bx is None: bx=x0
    bx=np.asarray(bx,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if bx.size!=x0.size: bx=x0
    return np.where(np.isfinite(bx),bx,0.0)
```
If time limit hits before first objective, best_x x0.

But `minimize` may call callback and if it raises TimeLimitError, does it catch? No, propagates. Good.

Full_objective code:
```
def full_objective(x,n,beta,state):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    z=x
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
       state maybe? return BAD, zeros
    # clip to Z_BOUND? L-BFGS bounds enforce but clip for safety
    z=np.clip(z, -15.0,15.0)
    p=softmax_z(z)
    c=fft_convolution(p)
    G,w,M=smooth_max_details(c,beta)
    if not np.isfinite(G): return BAD, np.zeros(n)
    f=2.0*n*G
    exact=2.0*n*M
    if np.isfinite(exact) and exact < state.get('best_exact', inf):
       state['best_p']=p.copy(); state['best_exact']=float(exact); state['best_z']=z.copy()
    s=corr_pw(p,w)
    g=2.0*s
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)): g=np.where(np.isfinite(g),g,0)
    gm=g.mean()
    grad=g-gm
    grad_z=p*grad
    if not finite: zeros
    return f, grad_z
```
`state.get` okay. But if best_exact inf, exact finite update. Use `inf=float('inf')`.

`param_objective` similar, but state best_p and maybe best_theta. For param, track z and p.

`block_objective` similar.

One subtlety: The smooth objective `G` approximates max(c). But if beta is low and M >0, G includes logZ/beta maybe >M. For L-BFGS, fine. For exact tracking, M exact.

Another subtlety: The evaluator's `max_b` uses `np.convolve` of list after clipping negative to 0 and min 1000. Our fast_score uses FFT clipped to nonnegative. For nonnegative p, FFT may have roundoff that makes max slightly different. Official at final decides.

Project simplex sorting implementation:
```
def project_simplex(v, target=1.0):
    v=np.asarray(v,dtype=np.float64)
    if v.size==0: return v
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)): return full(v.shape, target/v.size)
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    if u[0] <=0: return zeros? but target>0, need allocate. Actually if all v<=0, projection uniform? Formula: css top; if no rho? Let's handle.
    css=np.cumsum(u)
    k=np.arange(1,u.size+1,dtype=float)
    cond = u - (css - target)/k - 1e-12 >0?
```
Standard criterion: rho = max {k | u_k - (sum_{i=1..k} u_i - z)/k > 0}. Use `>0`. If none, projection all zeros? But target positive; if z small below sum all, rho at least 1 if u1>z? If z > u1, then all zeros impossible sum; actually formula with rho=0? If target <0? We ensure target>0 and v arbitrary. If z large > sum all, tau negative? Let's use binary search projection simpler robust. O(n*50) might be okay for polish few thousand iterations? For n=16384, 50*16k=820k; if 1000 iter, 820M numpy ops vectorized (max) maybe several seconds. Sorting 16k O(n log n) maybe 0.002s *1000=2s. Let's implement sorting robust.
If `rho` empty (no condition), set rho=0? The projection of v to simplex target with v all very negative: tau = -target/n? q=v-tau positive? Standard if no rho: tau = -target/n? Then q = v - tau, if all v <= tau? Hmm. Example v=[-10,-10], target1. Sort -1,-1. condition: u1 - (css1-1)/1 = -1 -(-1)=0 not >0; rho empty. Projection should zeros? But sum must 1; actually projection puts mass uniformly? For v=-10,-10, the closest point to v on simplex [1/k each] because v below simplex; tau = v_mean? Binary search gives q=[0.5,0.5]. Sorting formula: if no rho, set mu = -target/n? Let's derive: If all v_i <= mu, projection zeros except? For target>0, mu must be less than all v_i to make some positive; if no positive even at largest? The formula with no rho: mu = -target/n? Then q=v-mu; if v=-10, mu=-0.5, q=-9.5 negative. Need different. The standard algorithm when no rho: rho=0, mu=z/n? Let's check from projection: tau = (u[1:rho+1].sum()-z)/(rho+1) if rho>0; if rho=0, tau = -z/n? Wait for v all -10, target1, we want positive q. The active set should include all variables if v equal? Condition with >0 fails due equality maybe. Use `>=0`? If v=-10,-10, u1 - (u1-1)/1 =1 >0 actually! compute u1=-10, css1=-10, css-target=-11, (css-target)= -11, u - (css-target)= -10 - (-11)=1 >0. Sorry. rho exists. If v=[100,0], target1: u=[100,0], css-z: k1 99, u1-99=1>0; k2 sum=101, (100-1)/2=49.5, u2-49.5=-49.5, rho=1 tau=(100-1)=99, q=[1,0]. Good.
If v=[-1,-1], target1: k1: -1 -(-2)=1>0; k2: sum -2, css-z=-3, /2=-1.5, u2 -(-1.5)=0.5>0; rho=2 tau=(-2-1)/2=-1.5, q=0.5. Good. So condition works.
Implement:
```
rho_arr = np.nonzero(u - (css - target)/k > 0)[0]
if rho_arr.size==0:
   # should not happen; fallback uniform
   return full
rho = int(rho_arr[-1])
tau = (u[:rho+1].sum() - target)/(rho+1)
q = np.maximum(v - tau, 0.0)
s=q.sum(); if s>0: q*= target/s
else uniform
return q
```
This is O(n log n). Use only in polish/fallback, not many.

Now, `polish_gradient`:
```
def polish_gradient(p, deadline, beta=None):
    p=normalize_sequence(p)
    n=p.size
    if beta is None: beta=max(10000.0, 50.0*n)
    best_p=p.copy(); best_exact=fast_score(p)[0]
    current_f, _ = smooth_score(p,beta) # but we can compute in loop
    step=0.5
    no=0
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       c=fft_convolution(p); G,w,M=smooth
       f=2nG
       # update best exact if M lower
       exact=2nM
       if exact < best_exact: ...
       s=corr_pw(p,w); g=2*s; grad=g-g.mean()
       accepted=False
       for mult in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01):
           alpha=step*mult
           q=project_simplex(p - alpha*grad)
           cq=fft_convolution(q); Gq,_ ,Mq=smooth
           fq=2nGq
           if fq < f - 1e-12:
              p=q; f=fq; currentM=Mq; accepted=True; update; break
       if not accepted:
           step*=0.5
           no+=1
           if no>50 or step<1e-8: break
       else:
           step=min(2.0, step*1.15)
           no=0
       if it % 100 == 0: print
       it+=1
    return best_p,best_exact
```
This may be slow due project sort and extra FFT per trial. Use as final 30s. Fine.

Could omit line search and just accept if exact improves after small fixed step; but okay.

```
def run_param_search(n, deadline, rng, prev):
    starts = analytic_starts(n, rng, prev)
    top = score_top(starts, 5)
    if time.monotonic() >= deadline or not HAVE_SCRIPY: return top
    basis = build_basis(n, rng)
    d=basis.shape[0]
    inits=[]
    for p in top:
       theta = project_log_to_basis(basis,p)
       inits.append(theta)
    for scale in (0.5,1.0,2.0):
       inits.append(scale*rng.normal(0,1,d))
    # remove duplicates? not needed
    inits = inits[:6]
    bound=[(-8,8)]*d
    out=[]
    rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
    if rem<5: return top
    per=rem/len(inits)
    for theta in inits:
       p0 = normalize_sequence(softmax_z(basis @ theta))
       state={'best_p':p0,'best_exact':fast_score(p0)[0],'best_theta':theta.copy()}
       ctx={'deadline':time.monotonic()+per,'n':n,'beta':max(50000.0,5.0*n),'state':state}
       x=run_lbfgs_x(theta, bound, param_callback, ctx, maxiter=200)
       if state['best_p'] is not None: out.append(state['best_p'])
    if out:
       combined=top+out
       return score_top(combined,5)
    return top
```
But `beta=max(50000,5n)` for n=4096 =>50000 (beta*c~12), good. `per` maybe 5-10s. If d~12, L-BFGS very fast; maxiter 200 maybe fine.

`project_log_to_basis`:
```
def project_log_to_basis(basis,p):
    p=normalize_sequence(p)
    z=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300))
    z -= z.mean()
    # least squares
    try: theta=np.linalg.lstsq(basis,z,rcond=None)[0]
    except: theta=np.zeros(basis.shape[0])
    return np.clip(theta,-8,8)
```
No lambda.

`analytic_starts` as top-level. Need if prev is None. It includes random smooth uses rng. Use no lambda.

Functions:

`fft_convolution` as above.

`corr_pw`.

`smooth_max_details`.

`fast_score`:
```
def fast_score(p):
    p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64)
    n=p.size
    if n==0: return float('inf'), float('inf')
    s=float(np.sum(p))
    if s<=0 or not np.isfinite(s): return inf,inf
    c=fft_convolution(p)
    if c.size==0: return inf,inf
    M=float(np.max(c))
    if not finite: return inf,inf
    return float(2.0*n*M/(s*s)), M
```

`normalize_sequence`.

`z_from_p`, `softmax_z`.

`block_to_p`, `block_init_from_p`.

`project_simplex`, `smooth_score`, `polish_gradient`.

```
def add_fast_candidate(cands, score, p):
    if p is None: return
    if np.isfinite(score):
        cands.append([float(score), np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64).copy()])

def select_top_fast(cands, count): ... returns list p
```

Let's write each robustly.

`fft_convolution`: use `np.maximum(b,0.0)`. But if p has small negative due to? p nonnegative. If c contains tiny negative for boundary true zero, clipping okay. However, clipping all negatives can hide true negative if p has negative? We don't use negative. fine.

`corr_pw`: Since p,w nonnegative, s nonnegative. Roundoff negative clip.

`smooth_max_details`: Need handle `beta` maybe large; `x = beta * (b - M)`. If beta <=0 or M<=0, uniform weights. If `Z` is very large (active many), log(Z) maybe finite. If beta=1e6 and active 100, log100=4.6 /1e6 tiny.

`polish_gradient`: Need use `smooth_max_details` for current. In trial, computing smooth for each candidate; q normalized. Use `project_simplex` exact.

In `polish_gradient`, if `grad` norm too small break.
```
gnorm=np.linalg.norm(grad)
if gnorm<1e-14: break
```

`run_param_search`: If not HAVE_SCIPY, return top analytic. It uses `run_lbfgs_x`; if no scipy, just evaluates. Need `param_callback` top-level exists.

`refine_full`: If not HAVE_SCIPY, call polish_gradient for full deadline (skip block/lbfgs). This ensures some improvement even without scipy.

Block stage in refine: We use `block_objective` which uses `block_to_p`. If B=1? choose B>=2. Bounds list length B-1, if B=1 empty.

`block_init_from_p`: If B>n? not. L=n//B. `block_means` average. If last mean zero, use floor. `logm = np.log(np.maximum(block_means,1e-300)); rel = logm - logm[-1]; rel -= rel.max? no need; clip [-10,10]; return rel[:-1]`. `block_to_p` subtracts max and normalizes, so relative offset okay.

`full_objective`: `p = softmax_z(z)`. Note if z is all -15 due clip? softmax okay. We shift? softmax_z subtracts max so constant irrelevant. But if z from p has mean 0, okay.

`param_objective`: Need basis matrix. `z = basis @ theta` returns 1D. Clip? softmax handles. If theta large, z maybe large; clip in softmax? We can clip z to [-30,30] before softmax to avoid overflow, but softmax subtract max. If theta=8 and basis scaled max1, z range -? safe. But if basis not scaled? We'll scale basis to max abs 1, theta bound 8 => z range ±8*#terms? Actually sum of basis contributions can add up to d*8, maybe 96, exp overflow if max subtraction no (max 96, min -? exp(-192) underflow okay). `np.exp(-96)` fine. Use clip z [-50,50] in softmax.

`build_basis`: We scale each feature to max abs 1 and center. Good.

`analytic_starts`: Use `softmax_z` on z patterns. For random smooth, use `lowpass_profile(n,rng)`:
```
def lowpass_profile(n,rng):
    z=rng.normal(size=n)
    F=np.fft.rfft(z)
    fr=np.arange(F.size)
    cutoff=max(2,n//32)
    att=1.0/np.sqrt(1.0+(fr/cutoff)**2)
    F*=att
    z=np.fft.irfft(F,n)
    z-=z.mean()
    sd=float(np.std(z))
    if sd>0: z=3.0*z/sd
    return z
```
No closure.

`analytic_starts` returns list; maybe remove duplicates? not necessary.

`score_top`: If count > len, returns all.

`interpolate_profile`:
```
def interpolate_profile(p,new_n):
   p=normalize_sequence(p)
   if new_n==p.size: return p
   if p.size<2: return np.full(new_n,1/new_n)
   x_old=np.linspace(0,1,p.size)
   x_new=np.linspace(0,1,new_n)
   lp=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300))
   lp-=lp.mean()
   yn=np.interp(x_new,x_old,lp)
   q=np.exp(yn)
   q/=q.sum()
   return q
```
If x_new length large, okay.

```
    if not np.isfinite(f):
       f=BAD; g=np.zeros_like(x)
    if f > 1e90:
       f=BAD

       g=np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
```

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400.0):
    start=time.monotonic()
    try: budget=float(budget_s)
    except: budget=2400.0
    if budget<=0: return [1.0]
    try: seed=int(seed)
    except: seed=42
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    best_state={'best_seq':[1.0], 'best_score':float('inf'), 'eval_seconds':0.0}
    evaluate_and_store([1.0], best_state, 'fallback')
    prev_p=None
    try:
       prev=list(height_sequence_1)
       p=prev if ...; p_norm=normalize_sequence(prev)
       prev_p=p_norm
       evaluate_and_store([float(x) for x in p_norm], best_state, 'previous')
    except Exception: pass
    reserve=180.0 if budget>1200 else 90.0
    deadline=start+budget-reserve
    if deadline <= time.monotonic()+30:
       return best_state['best_seq']
    # param seed
    if budget < 300: seed_N=2048
    elif budget < 900: seed_N=2048
    else: seed_N=4096
    param_time=... (15,45,60)
    seed_deadline=min(deadline, time.monotonic()+param_time)
    p_seeds=run_param_search(seed_N, seed_deadline, rng, prev_p)
    if not p_seeds: p_seeds=[np.full(seed_N,1/seed_N)]
    p_seed=p_seeds[0]
    add_fast_candidate(cands, fast_score(p_seed)[0], p_seed) # maybe no official
    # If budget small, maybe refine at seed_N and return
    choose main_N:
      if budget < 400: main_N=seed_N; main_time=deadline-now
      elif budget < 1200: main_N=8192; reserve_upgrade=0
      else: main_N=16384; reserve_upgrade=200 if main_N<32768 else 0

       add_fast_candidate(cands, sc, p_ref)
```

```
def select_top_fast(cands, count):
    available=[]
    for item in cands:
       if item is not None: available.append([float(item[0]), item[1].copy()])
    selected=[]
    for _ in range(min(count, len(available))):
       bi=0
       for i in range(1,len(available)):
          if available[i][0] < available[bi][0]: bi=i
       selected.append([available[bi][0], available[bi][1]])
       del available[bi]
    return selected
```

```python
"""
Search for nonnegative sequences minimizing

    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / (sum(a))^2.

The objective is scale-invariant, so all internal sequences are normalized to
have sum one.  The program combines:

* a low-dimensional parametric search in a polynomial/cosine basis,
* coarse piecewise-constant block refinement,
* full-length smooth-max L-BFGS refinement in log-density variables,
* a short projected-gradient polish,
* adaptive use of larger sequence lengths,
* final authoritative evaluations and fallback to a valid score-2 sequence.

scipy is used when available; the code degrades to projected-gradient search.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
    HAVE_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    HAVE_SCIPY = False

BAD = 1e100
TARGET = 1.5027


class TimeLimitError(Exception):
    pass


def fft_convolution(a):
    """Linear convolution a*a via FFT, clipped to remove tiny negative roundoff."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([a[0] * a[0]], dtype=np.float64)

    need = 2 * n - 1
    size = 1
    while size < need:
        size <<= 1

    F = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    b = np.fft.irfft(F * F, size)[:need]
    b = np.ascontiguousarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    return np.maximum(b, 0.0)


def smooth_max_details(c, beta):
    """Smooth maximum log-sum-exp value, weights, and exact max."""
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    m = c.size
    if m == 0:
        return float("inf"), np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64), float("inf")

    M = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(M):
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64), float("inf")

    if M <= 0.0 or beta <= 0.0:
        w = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
        return 0.0, w, 0.0

    x = beta * (c - M)
    x = np.clip(x, -745.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(x)
    Z = float(np.sum(e))

    if Z <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(Z):
        e = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        e[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        Z = 1.0

    w = e / Z
    G = M + float(np.log(Z)) / beta
    return float(G), w, M


def corr_pw(p, w):
    """
    Return s_i = sum_j p_j w_{i+j}, used for the derivative
    d(max convolution)/d(p_i) = 2 * s_i.
    """
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    m = w.size
    if n == 0 or m == 0:
        return np.zeros(max(0, n), dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([float(np.dot(p, w[:1]))], dtype=np.float64)

    need = n + m - 1
    size = 1
    while size < need:
        size <<= 1

    P = np.fft.rfft(p, size)
    WR = np.fft.rfft(w[::-1], size)
    C = np.fft.irfft(P * WR, size)[:need]

    s = C[m - n : m][::-1].copy()
    return np.ascontiguousarray(np.maximum(s, 0.0), dtype=np.float64)


def fast_score(p):
    """Exact objective using FFT: (2*n*max(a*a))/(sum(a))^2."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf"), float("inf")

    S = float(np.sum(p))
    if S <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(S):
        return float("inf"), float("inf")

    c = fft_convolution(p)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf"), float("inf")

    M = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(M):
        return float("inf"), float("inf")

    return float(2.0 * n * M / (S * S)), M


def normalize_sequence(p):
    """Nonnegative normalization to sum 1."""
    arr = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return arr
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(arr))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        arr[:] = 1.0 / float(arr.size)
    else:
        arr *= 1.0 / s
    return arr


def softmax_z(z):
    """Stable softmax, guaranteed nonnegative and summing to one."""
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if z.size == 0:
        return z
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        return np.full(z.shape, 1.0 / float(z.size), dtype=np.float64)

    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0)
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(z.shape, 1.0 / float(z.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def z_from_p(p):
    """Log-density initialization from a positive simplex vector."""
    p = normalize_sequence(p)
    if p.size == 0:
        return p
    z = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    z -= float(np.mean(z))
    return np.clip(z, -15.0, 15.0)


def interpolate_profile(p, new_n):
    """Interpolate a probability profile in log-space."""
    p = normalize_sequence(p)
    new_n = int(max(1, new_n))
    if p.size == new_n:
        return p
    if p.size < 2:
        return np.full(new_n, 1.0 / float(new_n), dtype=np.float64)

    old_n = p.size
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old_n, dtype=np.float64)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n, dtype=np.float64)

    lp = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    lp -= float(np.mean(lp))
    yn = np.interp(x_new, x_old, lp)
    q = np.exp(np.clip(yn, -40.0, 40.0))
    s = float(np.sum(q))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        q = np.full(new_n, 1.0 / float(new_n), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        q *= 1.0 / s
    return q


def project_simplex(v, target=1.0):
    """Project onto {x >= 0, sum(x)=target} using the sorting algorithm."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return v.copy()
    if target <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
        return np.full(n, target / float(n), dtype=np.float64)

    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    css = np.cumsum(u)
    k = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    cond = u - (css - target) / k
    rho_idx = np.nonzero(cond > 1e-12)[0]

    if rho_idx.size == 0:
        return np.full(n, target / float(n), dtype=np.float64)

    rho = int(rho_idx[-1]) + 1
    tau = (float(np.sum(u[:rho])) - target) / float(rho)
    q = np.maximum(v - tau, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(q))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        q = np.full(n, target / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        q *= target / s
    return q


def smooth_score(p, beta):
    """Smooth objective 2*n*smooth_max(a*a) and exact max."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf"), float("inf")
    c = fft_convolution(p)
    G, _, M = smooth_max_details(c, beta)
    if not np.isfinite(G):
        return float("inf"), float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * G), M


def polish_gradient(p, deadline, beta):
    """
    Short projected-gradient polish on the simplex.  Used as a final local
    repair, especially when scipy is unavailable.
    """
    p = normalize_sequence(p)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return p, float("inf")
    if beta is None or beta <= 0.0:
        beta = max(100000.0, 50.0 * n)

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_exact, _ = fast_score(p)
    c = fft_convolution(p)
    G, w, M = smooth_max_details(c, beta)
    f = float(2.0 * n * G)
    step = 0.5
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if f > 1e90:
            break

        s = corr_pw(p, w)
        g = 2.0 * s
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
            g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
        grad = g - float(np.mean(g))
        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
        if not np.isfinite(gnorm) or gnorm < 1e-14:
            break

        accepted = False
        for mult in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.1, 0.05, 0.02):
            alpha = step * mult
            q = project_simplex(p - alpha * grad, 1.0)
            fq, Mq = smooth_score(q, beta)
            if np.isfinite(fq) and fq < f - 1e-13:
                p = q
                f = float(fq)
                c = fft_convolution(q)
                G, w, Mq = smooth_max_details(c, beta)
                accepted = True

                exact = float(2.0 * n * Mq)
                if exact < best_exact - 1e-15:
                    best_exact = exact
                    best_p = p.copy()
                    no_improve = 0
                break

        if not accepted:
            step *= 0.5
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 80 or step < 1e-8:
                break
        else:
            step = min(2.0, step * 1.15)

        it += 1
        if it % 50 == 0:
            print(f"polish it={it} exact={best_exact:.8f} step={step:.3e}")

    return best_p, best_exact


def block_to_p(n, B, c):
    """Convert relative block log-weights to a full constant-block sequence."""
    n = int(n)
    B = int(B)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if B <= 1:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)

    L = n // B
    if L <= 0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)

    z = np.zeros(B, dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    m = min(B - 1, c.size)
    if m > 0:
        z[:m] = c[:m]

    z -= float(np.max(z))
    wb = np.exp(z)
    s = float(np.sum(wb))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        wb = np.ones(B, dtype=np.float64) / float(B)
    else:
        wb /= s

    H = wb / float(L)
    return np.repeat(H, L)


def block_init_from_p(p, B):
    """Initial relative block log-weights from averaging p on blocks."""
    p = normalize_sequence(p)
    n = p.size
    B = int(B)
    L = n // B
    if B <= 1 or L <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    bm = p.reshape(B, L).mean(axis=1)
    bm = np.maximum(bm, 1e-300)
    lz = np.log(bm)
    rel = lz - lz[-1]
    rel -= float(np.max(rel))
    rel = np.clip(rel, -10.0, 10.0)
    return rel[:-1].copy()


def block_objective(x, n, B, beta, state):
    """Block-constant objective and gradient for smooth max objective."""
    n = int(n)
    B = int(B)
    if B <= 1:
        return float("inf"), np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    c = np.zeros(B - 1, dtype=np.float64)
    m = min(B - 1, x.size)
    c[:m] = x[:m]

    L = n // B
    p = block_to_p(n, B, c)
    full_c = fft_convolution(p)
    G, w, M = smooth_max_details(full_c, beta)
    if not np.isfinite(G):
        return BAD, np.zeros(B - 1, dtype=np.float64)

    exact = float(2.0 * n * M)
    if np.isfinite(exact) and exact < state.get("best_exact", float("inf")):
        state["best_p"] = p.copy()
        state["best_exact"] = exact
        state["best_c"] = c.copy()

    g = 2.0 * corr_pw(p, w)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
        g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)

    Gsum = g.reshape(B, L).sum(axis=1)
    z = np.zeros(B, dtype=np.float64)
    if m > 0:
        z[:m] = c[:m]
    z -= float(np.max(z))
    wb = np.exp(z)
    ws = float(np.sum(wb))
    if ws <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ws):
        wb = np.ones(B, dtype=np.float64) / float(B)
    else:
        wb /= ws

    Wavg = float(np.dot(wb, Gsum))
    grad_blocks = (wb / float(L)) * (Gsum - Wavg)
    grad = 2.0 * float(n) * grad_blocks[:-1]
    grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
    return float(2.0 * n * G), grad.astype(np.float64)


def block_callback(x, ctx):
    if time.monotonic() > ctx["deadline"]:
        raise TimeLimitError
    f, g = block_objective(x, int(ctx["n"]), int(ctx["B"]), float(ctx["beta"]), ctx["state"])
    return finalize_response(f, g, x, ctx)


def build_basis(n, rng=None):
    """
    Smooth low-dim feature matrix for log-density parametrization.
    Features are centered and scaled to max abs 1.
    """
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    c = x - 0.5

    raw = [
        c,
        c * c,
        c * c * c,
        (c * c) * (c * c),
        x * (1.0 - x),
        (x * x) * (1.0 - x) * (1.0 - x),
        np.cos(np.pi * x),
        np.sin(np.pi * x),
        np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x),
        np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x),
        1.0 - (2.0 * x - 1.0) ** 2,
    ]

    if rng is not None:
        raw.append(lowpass_profile(n, rng))

    feats = []
    for f in raw:
        f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        if f.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(f)):
            continue
        f -= float(np.mean(f))
        mx = float(np.max(np.abs(f)))
        if mx > 1e-12:
            f /= mx
            feats.append(f)

    if not feats:
        feats.append(c - float(np.mean(c)))

    basis = np.vstack(feats).astype(np.float64)
    return basis


def lowpass_profile(n, rng):
    """Random smooth profile via Fourier low-pass filtering."""
    if n <= 1:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    z = rng.normal(size=n)
    F = np.fft.rfft(z)
    fr = np.arange(F.size, dtype=np.float64)
    cutoff = max(2.0, float(n) / 32.0)
    att = 1.0 / np.sqrt(1.0 + (fr / cutoff) ** 2)
    F *= att
    z = np.fft.irfft(F, n)
    z -= float(np.mean(z))
    sd = float(np.std(z))
    if sd > 1e-12:
        z = 3.0 * z / sd
    return np.clip(z, -8.0, 8.0)


def project_log_to_basis(basis, p):
    """Least-squares projection of log density onto a feature basis."""
    p = normalize_sequence(p)
    d = basis.shape[0]
    if d <= 0 or p.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(max(0, d), dtype=np.float64)

    z = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    z -= float(np.mean(z))
    try:
        theta = np.linalg.lstsq(basis, z, rcond=None)[0]
    except Exception:
        theta = np.zeros(d, dtype=np.float64)
    return np.clip(theta, -8.0, 8.0)


def analytic_starts(n, rng, prev=None):
    """Diverse deterministic/random smooth initial profiles."""
    n = int(n)
    starts = []
    if n <= 0:
        return starts

    starts.append(np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64))

    if prev is not None:
        pp = normalize_sequence(prev)
        if pp.size == n:
            starts.append(pp.copy())
        elif pp.size > 0:
            starts.append(interpolate_profile(pp, n))

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    c = x - 0.5

    patterns = []
    for a in (-6.0, -4.0, -2.0, -1.0, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0, 6.0):
        patterns.append(a * c)
    for a in (-5.0, -2.5, 2.5, 5.0):
        patterns.append(a * c * c)
    for a in (-4.0, -2.0, 2.0, 4.0):
        patterns.append(a * np.cos(np.pi * x))
    for a in (-3.0, -1.5, 1.5, 3.0):
        patterns.append(a * (1.0 - (2.0 * x - 1.0) ** 2))
    for a in (-4.0, -2.0, 2.0, 4.0):
        patterns.append(a * x * (1.0 - x))
    for a in (-3.0, 3.0):
        patterns.append(a * np.abs(c))

    for z in patterns:
        if np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
            starts.append(softmax_z(z))

    for _ in range(3):
        z = lowpass_profile(n, rng)
        starts.append(softmax_z(z))

    return starts


def score_top(p_list, count):
    """Return top-count lower fast scores from a list of profiles."""
    scored = []
    for p in p_list:
        if p is None:
            continue
        pp = normalize_sequence(p)
        if pp.size == 0:
            continue
        sc, _ = fast_score(pp)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append([float(sc), pp])

    selected = []
    available = scored
    cnt = int(min(count, len(available)))
    for _ in range(cnt):
        bi = 0
        for i in range(1, len(available)):
            if available[i][0] < available[bi][0]:
                bi = i
        selected.append(available[bi][1].copy())
        del available[bi]
    return selected


def param_objective(theta, n, beta, basis, state):
    """Low-dimensional log-density objective and gradient."""
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = int(n)
    d = basis.shape[0]

    if theta.size != d:
        tmp = np.zeros(d, dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(d, theta.size)
        tmp[:m] = theta[:m]
        theta = tmp

    z = basis @ theta
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        return BAD, np.zeros(d, dtype=np.float64)

    p = softmax_z(z)
    c = fft_convolution(p)
    G, w, M = smooth_max_details(c, beta)
    if not np.isfinite(G):
        return BAD, np.zeros(d, dtype=np.float64)

    exact = float(2.0 * n * M)
    if np.isfinite(exact) and exact < state.get("best_exact", float("inf")):
        state["best_p"] = p.copy()
        state["best_exact"] = exact
        state["best_theta"] = theta.copy()

    g = 2.0 * corr_pw(p, w)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
        g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)

    gm = float(np.dot(p, g))
    grad_z = p * (g - gm)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_z)):
        grad_z = np.where(np.isfinite(grad_z), grad_z, 0.0)

    grad = basis.T @ grad_z
    grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
    return float(2.0 * n * G), grad.astype(np.float64)


def param_callback(x, ctx):
    if time.monotonic() > ctx["deadline"]:
        raise TimeLimitError
    f, g = param_objective(x, int(ctx["n"]), float(ctx["beta"]), ctx["basis"], ctx["state"])
    return finalize_response(f, g, x, ctx)


def full_objective(x, n, beta, state):
    """
    Full-length objective over z variables.  The objective and gradient are
    multiplied by n so L-BFGS sees O(1) gradients for typical 1/n densities.
    """
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = int(n)
    if x.size != n:
        tmp = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(n, x.size)
        tmp[:m] = x[:m]
        x = tmp

    z = np.clip(x, -15.0, 15.0)
    p = softmax_z(z)
    c = fft_convolution(p)
    G, w, M = smooth_max_details(c, beta)
    if not np.isfinite(G):
        return BAD, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    exact = float(2.0 * n * M)
    if np.isfinite(exact) and exact < state.get("best_exact", float("inf")):
        state["best_p"] = p.copy()
        state["best_exact"] = exact
        state["best_z"] = z.copy()

    g = 2.0 * corr_pw(p, w)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
        g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)

    gm = float(np.dot(p, g))
    grad_z = p * (g - gm)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_z)):
        grad_z = np.where(np.isfinite(grad_z), grad_z, 0.0)

    scale = float(n)
    f = scale * float(2.0 * n * G)
    grad = scale * grad_z
    return f, grad.astype(np.float64)


def full_callback(x, ctx):
    if time.monotonic() > ctx["deadline"]:
        raise TimeLimitError
    f, g = full_objective(x, int(ctx["n"]), float(ctx["beta"]), ctx["state"])
    return finalize_response(f, g, x, ctx)


def finalize_response(f, g, x, ctx):
    """Safe SciPy response and best-point tracking for time-limited LBFGS."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if not np.isfinite(f):
        f = BAD
    if f > 1e90:
        f = BAD

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
        g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)

    if np.isfinite(f) and f < ctx["best_f"] and f < 1e90:
        ctx["best_f"] = float(f)
        ctx["best_x"] = x.copy()

    return float(f), g.astype(np.float64)


def run_lbfgs_x(x0, bounds, callback, ctx, maxiter):
    """Run L-BFGS-B with a hard deadline and best-point recovery."""
    x0 = np.asarray(x0, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if x0.size == 0:
        return x0

    if bounds is not None:
        if len(bounds) != x0.size:
            bounds = None

    ctx["deadline"] = float(ctx.get("deadline", time.monotonic()))
    ctx["best_f"] = float("inf")
    ctx["best_x"] = x0.copy()

    if not HAVE_SCIPY:
        try:
            callback(x0, ctx)
        except Exception:
            pass
        bx = ctx.get("best_x", x0)
        return np.asarray(bx, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)

    options = {
        "maxiter": int(maxiter),
        "ftol": 1e-9,
        "gtol": 1e-10,
        "maxls": 60,
        "maxcor": 20,
    }

    xout = x0.copy()
    try:
        res = minimize(
            callback,
            x0,
            args=(ctx,),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            bounds=bounds,
            options=options,
        )
        if hasattr(res, "x") and res.x is not None:
            rb = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
            if rb.size == x0.size and np.all(np.isfinite(rb)):
                xout = rb
    except TimeLimitError:
        xout = ctx.get("best_x", x0)
    except Exception:
        xout = ctx.get("best_x", x0)

    xout = np.asarray(xout, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if xout.size != x0.size:
        xout = x0.copy()
    xout = np.where(np.isfinite(xout), xout, 0.0)
    return xout


def add_fast_candidate(cands, p, score=None):
    """Add a candidate to a fast-score candidate list."""
    if p is None:
        return
    pp = normalize_sequence(p)
    if pp.size == 0:
        return
    if score is None or not np.isfinite(score):
        sc, _ = fast_score(pp)
    else:
        sc = float(score)
    if np.isfinite(sc):
        cands.append([float(sc), pp.copy()])


def select_top_fast(cands, count):
    """Select top candidates by fast score, returning (score,p) pairs."""
    available = []
    for item in cands:
        if item is None or len(item) != 2:
            continue
        try:
            sc = float(item[0])
        except Exception:
            continue
        p = item[1]
        if p is None or not np.any(np.isfinite(p)):
            continue
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            available.append([sc, np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).copy()])

    selected = []
    cnt = int(min(count, len(available)))
    for _ in range(cnt):
        bi = 0
        for i in range(1, len(available)):
            if available[i][0] < available[bi][0]:
                bi = i
        selected.append([available[bi][0], available[bi][1].copy()])
        del available[bi]
    return selected


def evaluate_and_store(seq, state, label=""):
    """Call official evaluator and update best official state."""
    try:
        seq = [float(x) for x in seq]
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        val = float(evaluate_sequence(seq))
    except Exception:
        val = float("inf")
    dt = time.monotonic() - t0

    state["eval_seconds"] = float(state.get("eval_seconds", 0.0)) + float(dt)

    if np.isfinite(val) and val < float(state.get("best_score", float("inf"))):
        state["best_score"] = val
        state["best_seq"] = seq
        print(f"official {label}: score={val:.10f} n={len(seq)} eval_time={dt:.3f}")

    return val


def run_param_search(n, deadline, rng, prev=None):
    """
    Low-dimensional parametric search at a modest length.  Produces good
    initial profiles for larger refinement stages.
    """
    n = int(max(1, n))
    starts = analytic_starts(n, rng, prev)
    top = score_top(starts, 5)
    if not top:
        top = [np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)]

    if not HAVE_SCIPY or time.monotonic() >= deadline:
        return top

    basis = build_basis(n, rng)
    d = basis.shape[0]
    if d <= 0:
        return top

    inits = []
    for p in top:
        inits.append(project_log_to_basis(basis, p))

    for s in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 3.5):
        inits.append(s * rng.normal(size=d))

    inits = inits[:7]
    bounds = [(-8.0, 8.0)] * d
    beta = max(50000.0, 5.0 * float(n))

    out = []
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem < 4.0:
        return top

    per = min(30.0, rem / float(max(1, len(inits))))

    for theta in inits:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 2.0:
            break
        p0 = normalize_sequence(softmax_z(basis @ theta))
        sc0, _ = fast_score(p0)
        state = {
            "best_p": p0.copy(),
            "best_exact": float(sc0),
            "best_theta": theta.copy(),
        }
        ctx = {
            "deadline": min(deadline, time.monotonic() + per),
            "n": n,
            "beta": beta,
            "basis": basis,
            "state": state,
        }
        run_lbfgs_x(theta, bounds, param_callback, ctx, maxiter=300)
        if state.get("best_p") is not None:
            out.append(state["best_p"])

    combined = top + out
    return score_top(combined, 5)


def refine_full(p0, n, deadline, rng):
    """
    Full refinement pipeline for one target length:
    optional block L-BFGS, multi-stage full L-BFGS, projected polish.
    """
    p0 = normalize_sequence(p0)
    n = int(p0.size)
    if n == 0:
        return p0, float("inf")

    best_p = p0.copy()
    best_exact, _ = fast_score(p0)

    if not HAVE_SCIPY:
        return polish_gradient(best_p, deadline, max(200000.0, 50.0 * n))

    # ---------------- coarse block refinement ----------------
    if n >= 8192 and deadline - time.monotonic() > 120.0:
        choices = []
        for B in (128, 256, 512, 1024, 2048):
            if B < n and n % B == 0:
                choices.append(B)
        choices = choices[-2:]

        for B in choices:
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem < 35.0:
                break
            alloc = min(28.0, rem * 0.20)
            c0 = block_init_from_p(best_p, B)
            if c0.size == 0:
                continue

            state = {
                "best_p": best_p.copy(),
                "best_exact": float(best_exact),
                "best_c": c0.copy(),
            }
            ctx = {
                "deadline": time.monotonic() + alloc,
                "n": n,
                "B": B,
                "beta": max(50000.0, 10.0 * float(n)),
                "state": state,
            }
            bounds = [(-8.0, 8.0)] * c0.size
            run_lbfgs_x(c0, bounds, block_callback, ctx, maxiter=300)

            if state.get("best_exact", float("inf")) < best_exact - 1e-14:
                best_exact = float(state["best_exact"])
                best_p = state["best_p"].copy()
                print(f"block refine n={n} B={B} exact={best_exact:.8f}")

    # ---------------- full log-density L-BFGS ----------------
    z = z_from_p(best_p)
    bounds = [(-15.0, 15.0)] * n

    betas = [
        max(20000.0, 5.0 * float(n)),
        max(80000.0, 20.0 * float(n)),
        max(300000.0, 80.0 * float(n)),
        max(1000000.0, 300.0 * float(n)),
    ]
    fracs = [0.20, 0.30, 0.35, 0.15]

    polish_reserve = min(45.0, max(0.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.10))
    available = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - polish_reserve)

    for idx, beta in enumerate(betas):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= deadline - 15.0 or available <= 5.0:
            break

        frac = fracs[idx] if idx < len(fracs) else 0.1
        alloc = available * frac
        if alloc < 10.0:
            continue

        stage_end = now + alloc
        state = {
            "best_p": best_p.copy(),
            "best_exact": float(best_exact),
            "best_z": z.copy(),
        }
        ctx = {
            "deadline": stage_end,
            "n": n,
            "beta": beta,
            "state": state,
        }

        run_lbfgs_x(z, bounds, full_callback, ctx, maxiter=500)

        if state.get("best_exact", float("inf")) < best_exact - 1e-14:
            best_exact = float(state["best_exact"])
            best_p = state["best_p"].copy()
            print(f"full refine n={n} beta={beta:.3e} exact={best_exact:.8f}")

        # Always continue from the best exact point, not just smooth optimum.
        z = z_from_p(best_p)

    # ---------------- final projected polish ----------------
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 12.0:
        p2, sc2 = polish_gradient(best_p, time.monotonic() + min(45.0, rem),
                                  max(200000.0, 50.0 * float(n)))
        if sc2 < best_exact - 1e-14:
            best_exact = sc2
            best_p = p2
            print(f"after polish n={n} exact={best_exact:.8f}")

    return best_p, best_exact


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400.0):
    """
    Main search function.  Returns a list of nonnegative floats.
    """
    t_start = time.monotonic()

    try:
        seed = int(seed)
    except Exception:
        seed = 42

    try:
        budget_s = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget_s = 2400.0

    if budget_s <= 0.0:
        return [1.0]

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    best_state = {
        "best_seq": None,
        "best_score": float("inf"),
        "eval_seconds": 0.0,
    }

    # Safe score-2 fallback.
    fallback_p = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    evaluate_and_store([float(x) for x in fallback_p], best_state, "fallback")
    cands = []
    add_fast_candidate(cands, fallback_p, 2.0)

    # Use any provided previous construction as a start and fallback.
    prev_p = None
    try:
        raw_prev = height_sequence_1
    except Exception:
        raw_prev = None

    if raw_prev is not None:
        try:
            pp = normalize_sequence(raw_prev)
            if pp.size > 0:
                prev_p = pp
                seq_prev = [float(x) for x in pp]
                evaluate_and_store(seq_prev, best_state, "previous")
                add_fast_candidate(cands, pp)
        except Exception:
            pass

    reserve = 240.0 if budget_s > 1500.0 else 120.0
    deadline = t_start + max(0.0, budget_s - reserve)

    if deadline <= time.monotonic() + 30.0:
        if best_state.get("best_seq") is not None:
            return list(best_state["best_seq"])
        return [1.0]

    # ---------------- low-dimensional seed search ----------------
    if budget_s < 300.0:
        seed_N = 2048
    elif budget_s < 900.0:
        seed_N = 2048
    else:
        seed_N = 4096

    param_time = 15.0 if budget_s < 300.0 else (45.0 if budget_s < 900.0 else 65.0)
    seed_deadline = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + param_time)

    p_seeds = run_param_search(seed_N, seed_deadline, rng, prev_p)
    if not p_seeds:
        p_seeds = [np.full(seed_N, 1.0 / float(seed_N), dtype=np.float64)]

    for p in p_seeds[:3]:
        add_fast_candidate(cands, p)

    # ---------------- main length selection ----------------
    if budget_s < 400.0:
        main_N = seed_N
    elif budget_s < 1200.0:
        main_N = 8192
    else:
        main_N = 16384

    upgrade_time = 0.0
    upgrade_N = 0
    if budget_s >= 700.0 and main_N < 8192:
        upgrade_N = min(32768, max(2 * main_N, 8192))
        upgrade_time = 120.0
    elif budget_s >= 1200.0 and main_N < 16384:
        upgrade_N = min(32768, max(2 * main_N, 16384))
        upgrade_time = 260.0
    elif budget_s >= 1800.0 and main_N < 32768:
        upgrade_N = min(32768, max(2 * main_N, 32768))
        upgrade_time = min(650.0, 0.28 * max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic()))

    main_time = max(10.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) - upgrade_time)
    main_deadline = time.monotonic() + main_time

    # Build main-length starts.
    main_starts = []
    for p in p_seeds[:6]:
        main_starts.append(interpolate_profile(p, main_N))
    if prev_p is not None:
        main_starts.append(interpolate_profile(prev_p, main_N))

    # Add a few smooth random perturbations of the current best seeds.
    for p in p_seeds[:2]:
        try:
            pz = interpolate_profile(p, main_N)
            noise = lowpass_profile(main_N, rng)
            perturbed = normalize_sequence(pz * (1.0 + 0.12 * noise))
            main_starts.append(perturbed)
        except Exception:
            pass

    main_starts = score_top(main_starts, 3)
    if not main_starts:
        main_starts = [np.full(main_N, 1.0 / float(main_N), dtype=np.float64)]

    main_cands = []
    if len(main_starts) == 1:
        allocs = [main_time]
    elif len(main_starts) == 2:
        allocs = [0.65 * main_time, 0.35 * main_time]
    else:
        allocs = [0.55 * main_time, 0.30 * main_time, 0.15 * main_time]

    for i, p0 in enumerate(main_starts):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= main_deadline - 20.0:
            break
        alloc = allocs[i] if i < len(allocs) else 0.0
        if alloc < 25.0:
            break

        stage_deadline = min(main_deadline, now + alloc)
        try:
            p_ref, sc_ref = refine_full(p0, main_N, stage_deadline, rng)
            add_fast_candidate(main_cands, p_ref, sc_ref)
            add_fast_candidate(cands, p_ref, sc_ref)
        except Exception:
            pass

        if np.isfinite(sc_ref) and sc_ref < 1.45:
            break

    # ---------------- optional upgrade to larger length ----------------
    if upgrade_N > main_N and upgrade_time > 60.0:
        top_main = select_top_fast(main_cands, 1)
        if top_main:
            p_upgrade_start = interpolate_profile(top_main[0][1], upgrade_N)
        else:
            p_upgrade_start = interpolate_profile(p_seeds[0], upgrade_N)

        # A quick official evaluation of the interpolated profile can be useful,
        # but only if it is unlikely to be too expensive.
        if best_state.get("eval_seconds", 0.0) < 90.0 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 60.0:
            seq_up0 = [float(x) for x in p_upgrade_start]
            evaluate_and_store(seq_up0, best_state, f"interpolated n={upgrade_N}")
            add_fast_candidate(cands, p_upgrade_start)

        up_deadline = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + upgrade_time)
        if up_deadline > time.monotonic() + 45.0:
            try:
                p_ref, sc_ref = refine_full(p_upgrade_start, upgrade_N, up_deadline, rng)
                add_fast_candidate(cands, p_ref, sc_ref)
            except Exception:
                pass

    # ---------------- final selection ----------------
    top = select_top_fast(cands, 5)

    # Officially evaluate the best fast candidates if time permits.
    for i, item in enumerate(top):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= deadline - 20.0 and i > 0:
            break

        sc, p = item
        p = normalize_sequence(p)
        n = p.size
        if n <= 0:
            continue

        # Keep 32768 only when we have time; it should already be evaluated above
        # in most cases, but avoid crunching into the final reserve.
        if n >= 32768 and now >= deadline - 80.0:
            # If this is the overall best fast candidate, still allow one final
            # evaluation because larger N is often necessary to beat the target.
            if i > 0:
                continue

        seq = [float(x) for x in p]
        try:
            off = evaluate_and_store(seq, best_state, f"final n={n}")
            if np.isfinite(off):
                sc = off
        except Exception:
            pass

        if top and top[0][1].size == n:
            break

    # Choose between the best officially evaluated sequence and the best fast
    # sequence.  If the top fast sequence is at a safe length, return it even if
    # it was not officially evaluated during this run.
    if top:
        best_fast_score, best_fast_p = top[0][0], normalize_sequence(top[0][1])
        if best_state.get("best_seq") is None:
            return [float(x) for x in best_fast_p]

        # If the top fast candidate is no larger than 16384, it is a safe choice
        # even without an official score if its fast score beats the best official.
        if best_fast_p.size <= 16384 and best_fast_score < float(best_state["best_score"]) - 1e-9:
            return [float(x) for x in best_fast_p]

        # If the top fast candidate is larger and its fast score is much better,
        # return it only when we have no serious official candidate or it has
        # already been officially evaluated.
        if best_fast_p.size > 16384:
            seq_id = best_state.get("best_seq")
            same_len = (isinstance(seq_id, list) and len(seq_id) == best_fast_p.size)
            if (
                best_fast_score < float(best_state["best_score"]) - 1e-9
                and not same_len
                and float(best_state.get("best_score", float("inf"))) > 1.6
            ):
                # Only risk unscored 32768 if our official budget is unused.
                if best_state.get("eval_seconds", 0.0) < 180.0:
                    return [float(x) for x in best_fast_p]

    if best_state.get("best_seq") is not None:
        return list(best_state["best_seq"])

    return [1.0]
```
